### [1] [CORAL-KV: Compact Cross-Layer Prediction and Residual Coding for Autoregressive Video KV Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12514)
*Albert Ge,Akash Haridas,Utkarsh Saxena,Ruijing Yang,Ian Colbert,Frederic Sala,Vikram Appia,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Coral-KV 通过跨层预测残差量化压缩自回归视频扩散模型的 KV 缓存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型需维护不断增长的注意力 KV 缓存，内存成为长视频生成的瓶颈。现有基于聚类的量化方法仅利用层内相似性，且后续帧关注压缩历史时，小的重建误差会随视频增长而累积。

Method: 提出 Coral-KV，利用每层缓存状态预测后续层的键和值，再对预测残差量化。构造紧凑的分块对角加低秩（block-DPLR）预测器：分块对角项建模每个注意力头内的通道关系，全局低秩项建模头间依赖。支持复用预测器或针对当前视频重拟合轻量分块对角预测器。

Result: 在 HY-WorldPlay 和 MAGI-1 上，可复用配置相比 QVG-4 分别提升 PSNR 0.32 dB 和 4.95 dB，缓存内存减少 48% 和 37%；视频适配配置相比 QVG-Pro 在 MAGI-1 上提升 PSNR 3.77 dB，内存减少 33.7%。

Conclusion: Coral-KV 在长视频生成中保持更高重建保真度，减少时间不连续，并维持相当的生成延迟。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models maintain a growing attention key-value (KV) cache, making memory a key bottleneck for long video generation. Recently, clustering-based quantization has emerged as an effective method to compress video KV caches by grouping similar key and value vectors then quantizing residual errors. However, when later frames attend to the compressed history, small reconstruction errors can compound as the video grows. Beyond the within-layer similarity exploited by clustering, we find strong dependencies across cache layers in video models. This motivates Coral-KV - Calibrated Offline, Residual-coded, and Adapted Locally - a KV-cache compression framework that uses cache states in each layer to predict keys and values in subsequent layers, then quantizes the remaining prediction residuals. We construct compact block-diagonal-plus-low-rank (block-DPLR) predictors: block-diagonal components model channel relationships within each attention head, while a global low-rank term models dependencies between heads. Coral-KV exposes a flexible fidelity-memory trade-off: it can reuse its predictors across videos or refit lightweight block-diagonal predictors to the current video for higher fidelity. Across extensive evaluations on both HY-WorldPlay and MAGI-1, our reusable configuration improves PSNR over QVG-4 by 0.32 dB and 4.95 dB, respectively, while using 48% and 37% less cache memory. Against QVG-Pro, our video-adapted configuration improves MAGI-1 PSNR by 3.77 dB while using 33.7% less cache memory. Coral-KV also maintains higher reconstruction fidelity over extended generation and reduces temporal discontinuities while retaining comparable generation latency.

</details>


### [2] [TraceICL: Thread-Resolved Demonstration Curation with Consistency Calibration for Robust Multimodal In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12516)
*Yigit Yilmaz,Elena Petrova,Mehmet Kaya,Lucia Rossi,Amir Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的 AVA 框架，将交错多模态上下文学习（ICL）的示例流程解耦为三个可组合阶段，在五个视觉语言基准上平均提升最强同设定基线 +1.7 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的大视觉语言模型（LVLM）在交错多模态 ICL 中虽能从少量图文-答案示例中获取新任务，但仍很脆弱：示例与错误图像绑定、冗余示例挤占有效示例、噪声答案被逐字复制进预测。因此需要一种无需训练的方法来系统化地组织示例流程。

Method: AVA 由三个可组合模块组成：1) Thread-Resolved Decomposer (TRD) 将交错上下文切分为逐样本线程并以显式边界重新拼接，恢复图文绑定；2) Influence-scored Demonstration Selector (IDS) 通过一次额外前向估计每个候选示例的边际影响力，去除冗余并按价值降序排序；3) Weak-to-Strong Consistency Calibration (WSC) 用弱同级模型的重建置信度对每个示例打分，并在解码时软性重加权其贡献。全程不计算梯度，LVLM 保持冻结。

Result: 在五个视觉语言基准、交错 4-shot 提示下，AVA 相比最强同设定基线平均提升 +1.7 分，VizWiz 上最高提升 +2.4 分，缩小了 ScienceQA-IMG 上的 require-visual 性能差距，并在 30% 噪声示例下保留大部分增益；延迟开销为 26%，可通过离线缓存摊销。

Conclusion: AVA 以免训练、无梯度的方式有效缓解了多模态 ICL 的示例绑定、冗余与噪声问题，在多个基准上取得稳定提升且延迟可控，说明对示例流程的结构化组织是提升冻结 LVLM 上下文学习鲁棒性的有效途径。

Abstract: Interleaved multimodal in-context learning (ICL) lets frozen large vision-language models (LVLMs) acquire new tasks from a handful of image-text-answer demonstrations, yet it remains fragile: demonstrations bind to the wrong images, redundant exemplars crowd out informative ones, and noisy answers are copied verbatim into predictions. We present AVA, a training-free framework that organizes the demonstration pipeline of multimodal ICL into three composable stages. A Thread-Resolved Decomposer (TRD) segments the interleaved context into per-sample threads and re-stitches them with explicit boundaries, restoring image-text binding. An Influence-scored Demonstration Selector (IDS) estimates each candidate's marginal influence with one extra forward pass, removes redundancy, and orders the survivors by descending value. A Weak-to-Strong Consistency Calibration (WSC) module scores each demonstration by a weak sibling model's reconstruction confidence and softly reweights its contribution during decoding. On five vision-language benchmarks with interleaved 4-shot prompts, AVA improves the strongest same-setting baseline by +1.7 points on average, with gains of up to +2.4 points on VizWiz, shrinks the require-visual performance gap on ScienceQA-IMG, and retains most of its gain under 30% noisy demonstrations, at a 26% latency overhead that an offline cache amortizes. No gradients are ever computed; the LVLM stays frozen.

</details>


### [3] [TerraAgent: Demonstration-Grounded Multi-Agent Reasoning for Long-Context Remote Sensing Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12517)
*Jingwen Huang,Yiming Fang,Hanxiao Wu,Boyuan He,Keyi Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架 AVA，通过演示检索、证据分解与反事实审计，提升遥感视觉问答的准确率与图像依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感多模态大模型（RS MLLMs）主要以准确率评估，无法判别正确答案是否真正依赖图像证据，"先验作答"不可见。同时跨 GSD、传感器与城市场景的演示匹配困难，长视觉上下文使证据稀释、错误在多步推理中传播。

Method: 免训练框架 AVA，包裹冻结的 RS MLLM，含三模块：GDR 按 GSD 分桶、基于场景图检索演示，并过滤答案不被图像支持的演示；MACE 将查询分解为带证据类型的子问题 DAG，路由至合适感知工具，并在共享证据账本中记录可验证来源，带有限重感知；CVA 对引用证据做反事实扰动（消融与替换）审计最终答案，计算视觉依赖分数用于置信度校准与强制取证重查询。

Result: 在 RSVQA-LR/HR、VRSBench、LHRS-Bench 上，平均准确率较最强同设置基线提升至多 2.40 分；视觉依赖分数由 0.28 升至 0.49；纯先验作答率由 46.2% 降至 21.7%；幻觉率由 17.1% 降至 9.8%；成本为单次骨干前向的 2.6 倍，且无需微调。

Conclusion: AVA 在不微调前提下，通过演示过滤、证据化推理与反事实审计，同时提升准确率、图像依赖性与可靠性，并抑制纯先验作答与幻觉。

Abstract: Remote sensing visual question answering (RS-VQA) requires answers that truly depend on image evidence, yet current remote sensing multimodal large language models (RS MLLMs) are still evaluated mainly by accuracy, which cannot reveal whether a correct answer was produced without using the image. This setting introduces three challenges: demonstrations retrieved across different ground sample distances (GSD), sensors, and city textures may be poorly matched; real queries often involve long visual contexts from multi-scale tiles, multi-temporal frames, and retrieved knowledge, where evidence can be diluted and errors can propagate through multi-step reasoning; and prior-only answering remains invisible to standard metrics. We present AVA, a training-free framework that wraps a frozen RS MLLM with three modules. GDR performs GSD-bucketed, scene-graph-based demonstration retrieval and filters demonstrations whose answers are unsupported by their images. MACE decomposes each query into an evidence-typed sub-question DAG, routes sub-questions to suitable perception tools, and records verified provenance in a shared evidence ledger with bounded re-perception. CVA audits the final answer through counterfactual perturbations of cited evidence, including ablation and swap, and computes a visual-dependency score for confidence calibration and evidence-forcing re-query. On RSVQA-LR/HR, VRSBench, and LHRS-Bench, AVA improves average accuracy by up to 2.40 points over the strongest same-setting baselines, raises the visual-dependency score from 0.28 to 0.49, reduces the prior-only answer rate from 46.2 percent to 21.7 percent, and lowers the hallucination rate from 17.1 percent to 9.8 percent, with 2.6x the cost of a single backbone forward pass and no fine-tuning.

</details>


### [4] [MIRAGE: Metric Inference and Reliability-Aware Gaussian Estimation from Feed-Forward Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12568)
*Xinzhe Wang,Changjian Jiang,Kaiwen Song,Xudong Li,Kerui Ren,Ran Yi,Lizhuang Ma,Chunhua Shen,Linning Xu,Tao Lu,Mulin Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MIRAGE，将前馈视觉几何锚定到 LiDAR-IMU 度量运动，并按对应支撑与跨模态一致性调节在线位姿修正，实现度量精度的位姿估计与高保真高斯重建，并构建新的长时序室内单目重建基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈三维先验虽能从图像流实现高质量重建，但缺乏可靠度量尺度且存在长期轨迹漂移；LiDAR-惯性估计虽具度量精度，却缺少重建所需的图像空间一致性。同时，大规模室内长时序单目重建缺少基准数据。

Method: MIRAGE 以前馈视觉几何为基，将其锚定到 LiDAR-IMU 的度量运动，并根据对应关系支撑度与跨模态一致性对在线位姿修正进行调节，从而联合优化度量位姿估计与高斯重建；此外搭建多模态采集平台以生成新基准的 6-DoF 参考轨迹。

Result: 在 Oxford Spires、M2DGR 和 INS 上取得准确的度量轨迹估计，优于计算代价高昂的 COLMAP；并在新建的长时序室内单目基准上系统评测了近期单目大规模重建方法。

Conclusion: 跨模态锚定与一致性调节能同时提升度量位姿精度与高斯重建保真度，并为长时序室内单目重建提供可信参考轨迹与评测基准。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D priors enable high-quality reconstruction from image streams but often lack reliable metric scale and suffer from long-term trajectory drift. In contrast, LiDAR--inertial estimation provides metrically accurate motion but lacks the image-space consistency needed for high-fidelity reconstruction. We propose $MIRAGE$, which anchors feed-forward visual geometry to LiDAR--IMU metric motion and regulates online pose corrections according to correspondence support and cross-modal agreement, jointly enabling accurate metric pose estimation and high-fidelity Gaussian reconstruction. Experiments on Oxford Spires, M2DGR and INS show that MIRAGE achieves accurate metric trajectory estimation, outperforming COLMAP, a widely used but computationally expensive reference for vision-only benchmarks. Motivated by the scarcity of long-horizon monocular benchmarks for large-scale indoor reconstruction, we further build a multimodal data-collection platform and use MIRAGE to establish accurate 6-DoF reference trajectories for a new vision-only benchmark. We benchmark recent monocular large-scale reconstruction methods on this dataset, providing a systematic evaluation of their performance in long-horizon indoor scenes.

</details>


### [5] [Scaling Multi-Teacher Distillation for Digital Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12574)
*Sofiène Boutaj,Pierre Marza,Varun Belagali,Dimitris Samaras,Maria Vakalopoulou,Stergios Christodoulidis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出改进的多教师蒸馏（MTD）方法，通过学习教师特定表征，使学生编码器即便在教师数量增加时也能有效扩展，从而以最高10倍更高效的紧凑模型匹配甚至超越大型教师基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多教师蒸馏可从多个教师网络向单个学生网络迁移知识，在数字病理等领域很有前景，因为近期出现了许多强大的基础模型。但作者发现，标准多教师蒸馏方法在增加教师数量时表现不佳，难以有效扩展。

Method: 作者指出学习教师特定表征是MTD扩展的关键，并提出多项设计选择：可学习的教师token、定制的注意力机制、额外的混合专家层以及对比损失，以更好地学习教师特定表征。

Result: 该方法在教师数量增加时表现更优，可训练出比大型教师基础模型最高高效10倍的紧凑学生编码器，同时在大规模切片级和全切片级任务上匹配甚至超越教师模型。作者在39个任务上评估了10多个基础模型，并发布了OMNI系列基础模型，由10个先进基础模型的知识训练而成。

Conclusion: 作者分析了学习到的教师特定表征，强调其互补性，并解释了为何这些表征在下游任务中易于聚合；最终验证了通过教师特定表征实现可扩展多教师蒸馏的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-teacher distillation allows transferring knowledge from multiple "teacher" networks to a single "student" network. This method is promising in fields such as digital pathology, where many powerful foundation models were proposed recently. However, as we show in this paper, the standard multi-teacher distillation approach reacts poorly to an increase in the number of teachers used to train a student encoder. We demonstrate that learning teacher-specific representations is a key to scaling in MTD. Importantly, different design choices, such as learnable teacher tokens, a tailored attention scheme, additional mixture-of-experts layers and a contrastive loss, are proposed to better learn such teacher-specific representations. We show that our method better scales with respect to the number of considered teachers, allowing us to train compact student encoders, up to 10x more efficient than larger teacher foundation models, while matching their performance and even outperforming them on a large set of tile-level and slide-level tasks. We conduct a thorough empirical validation, evaluating more than 10 foundation models on 39 tasks spanning tile and slide levels. As a result, we release a new collection of strong and efficient foundation models, named OMNI, trained from the knowledge of 10 state-of-the-art foundation models. We finally conduct an analysis of the learned teacher-specific representations, highlighting their complementarity and explaining why they can be easily aggregated at downstream time.

</details>


### [6] [MotionCoach: Constructing Multimodal Guidance for Video Professional Motion Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12963)
*Siyi Qian,Hanqing Yang,Weihuang Lin,Zihao Yang,Yichen Lu,Yuanhong Zheng,Sheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MotionCoach——一个多轮工具调用并自我验证的多模态智能体，为视频编辑模型生成"文字提示+参考片段"的多模态指导，实现专业动作编辑，并发布基准 VPMEBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业动作（如表演、体育、武术）涉及精细的动作为技巧与时序结构，但现有视频编辑模型在仅有专业动作名称、缺乏详细描述时往往生成错误结果（如 Stoppie 指令下错误抬起前轮）。核心挑战在于如何在不重训视频编辑模型的前提下，向模型注入外部专业动作知识。

Method: 1) MotionCoach：带多轮工具调用与自我验证的多模态智能体，产出有依据的提示词（grounded prompt）与参考片段作为充分可靠的多模态指导；2) 数据：构建数据管线生成轨迹，在 MotionCoach-SFT-10k 上冷启动，在 MotionCoach-RL-2k 上应用 GRPO，奖励涵盖动作指导提示、动作结果视频与格式三方面；3) 评测：提出 VPMEBench，含 314 个专业动作编辑任务及细粒度参考与标注。

Result: Seedance 2.0 上总体分数从 55.1 提升至 65.6，MiniMax-H3 上从 51.2 提升至 64.5。表明学会构建多模态指导即可实现精确的专业动作编辑，无需重训视频编辑模型。

Conclusion: 通过智能体式多模态指导构建（提示词+参考片段）替代模型再训练，缓解了专业动作名称缺乏细粒度描述的知识鸿沟，为专业动作视频编辑提供了可扩展的即插即用方案与配套基准。

Abstract: Video editing models achieve strong performance in modifying appearance, backgrounds, styles, and motions, empowered by large-scale training data. Beyond general scenarios, professional motions are significant in performance, sports, and martial arts. This paper focuses on professional motion editing, which involves fine-grained motion technique and temporal structure. Given professional motion names but lacking detailed descriptions, existing models often fail to generate professional motions, such as incorrectly lifting the front wheel under a Stoppie instruction. To address this challenge, we introduce MotionCoach, a trained multimodal agent that uses multi-turn tools and performs self-verification, producing a grounded prompt and a reference clip as reliable and sufficient multimodal guidance to inject external professional motion knowledge into video editing models. To supply training data, we build a data pipeline to construct trajectories. We cold-start the agent on MotionCoach-SFT-10k and apply Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) on MotionCoach-RL-2k with rewards on the motion guidance prompt, the motion outcome video, and the format. For evaluation, we introduce VPMEBench, comprising 314 professional motion editing tasks with fine-grained references and annotations. Extensive experiments show that MotionCoach improves the overall score from 55.1 to 65.6 on Seedance 2.0 and from 51.2 to 64.5 on MiniMax-H3. Learning to construct multimodal guidance enables precise professional motion editing without retraining video editing models.

</details>


### [7] [Mind the Temporal Gap: Evaluating Video Models for Temporal Understanding in Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12575)
*Shiva Sreeram,Erfan Aasi,Tsun-Hsuan Wang,Alaa Maalouf,Daniela Rus*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文通过受控的自运动任务检验视频语言模型（VLM）在驾驶等安全关键场景下是否真正具备时间动态推理能力，发现提示方法不可靠且存在类别偏置，而轻量微调与中间特征探针能恢复强运动判别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频语言模型日益被视为通用视频推理器，但其在驾驶等安全关键场景中能否稳健捕捉时间动态尚不清楚，本文旨在系统检验这一能力假设。

Method: 设计受控的自运动（ego-motion）任务，即从驾驶视频中识别动作；系统评测多种提示策略（标准提示、视觉提示、少样本提示、思维链提示），并对比轻量 LoRA 微调以及在中间视觉特征上训练简单探针（probes）的效果。

Result: 各类提示方法表现不可靠，高度依赖任务与模型，并存在持续的类别偏置；较新模型在特定任务上通过少样本提示获得显著提升。相比之下，LoRA 微调与中间视觉特征探针能恢复强运动判别性能，说明运动相关线索常存在于模型内部，却未被最终的视频到语言通路可靠利用。

Conclusion: 在驾驶场景中，当前视频语言模型仍是不靠谱的时间推理器，更像帧级、偏置驱动的识别器，而非可靠的时间世界模型；其内部蕴含运动信息但未能被有效调用。

Abstract: Modern video-language models are increasingly treated as general-purpose video reasoners, yet it remains unclear whether they robustly capture temporal dynamics in safety-critical settings such as driving. We study this question through controlled ego-motion tasks that require identifying actions from driving videos. Across video-language models, we find that standard prompting, visual prompting, few-shot prompting, and chain-of-thought prompting remain unreliable and highly task- and model-dependent, with persistent class biases, although newer models show substantial task-specific few-shot gains. In contrast, lightweight LoRA finetuning and simple probes trained on intermediate visual features recover strong motion-discriminative performance, indicating that motion-relevant cues are often present inside the models but are not reliably used by the final video-to-language pathway. These results suggest that, in driving, current video-language models remain unreliable temporal reasoners and often behave more like frame-level, bias-driven recognizers than dependable temporal world models.

</details>


### [8] [CV-OPD: Contrastive Vision On-policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13264)
*Haowei Wang,Hao Lin,Qi Zhu,Jiayi Ji,Deyi Ji,Liujuan Cao,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现on-policy distillation (OPD)会使学生VLM依赖答案捷径而非视觉证据，提出CV-OPD，通过对比损失与差异门控纠正该捷径，在细粒度视觉基准上显著提升视觉推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到异常现象：经OPD蒸馏的VLM即便在相关视觉区域被遮挡或替换为噪声时，仍能准确作答。定量与定性实验表明，以答案为提示的OPD未能真正教会学生寻找并依赖视觉证据；理论分析指出，该失败源于蒸馏过程中缺乏视觉约束而产生的捷径（shortcut）。

Method: 提出Contrastive Vision On-policy Distillation (CV-OPD)：在相同学生前缀条件下，对比正、负裁剪所诱导的教师分布，并加入margin-based对比约束，且该约束按token级与样本级差异进行调制。具体包括：(1) 引入与答案无关的负教师，设计Contrastive Divergence Loss (CDL)，通过对比正负教师约束学生特征分布，纠正其对视觉区域的利用方式；(2) 引入Discrepancy Aware Gate (DAG)，利用token级与样本级差异筛选视觉相关token进行优化，引导学生注意力聚焦视觉内容。

Result: 在广泛细粒度视觉基准上的大量实验表明，CV-OPD显著缓解了捷径问题，并持续提升现有VLM的视觉推理能力。

Conclusion: OPD因缺少视觉约束而存在捷径缺陷，CV-OPD通过对比式正负教师约束与差异感知门控有效弥补该缺陷，为VLM后训练中真正依赖视觉证据的蒸馏提供了可行方案。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) reveals versatility and convenience during VLM post-training, providing dense supervision for student from teacher. However, we observed an anomaly that the distilled VLM could still answer the questions accurately even when the related visual regions were masked or replaced with noise. Building on this observation, our quantitative and qualitative experiments demonstrate that OPD, which uses the answer as a hint, has failed to teach students to genuinely seek out and rely on visual evidence. Our theoretical analysis suggests that this failure arises from a shortcut enabled by the lack of visual constraints during distillation. To address the issue, we introduce Contrastive Vision On-policy Distillation (CV-OPD), which compares teacher distributions induced by positive and negative crops while conditioning on the same student prefix and augments optimization with a margin-based contrastive constraint, which is modulated with discrepancy gating across token and sample level. Specifically, CV-OPD first introduces a negative teacher unrelated to the answer and designs a Contrastive Divergence Loss (CDL) that constrains the feature distribution of student by contrasting the positive and negative teachers, correcting how the student exploits visual regions. The CV-OPD introduces a Discrepancy Aware Gate (DAG) that uses token-level and sample-level discrepancies to select visually relevant tokens for optimization, directing the student's attention toward visual content. Extensive experiments across a broad range of fine-grained visual benchmarks demonstrate that CV-OPD substantially alleviates shortcut and consistently improves the visual reasoning capabilities of existing VLMs.

</details>


### [9] [MemReg: Streaming Outdoor LiDAR Point Cloud Registration with Hybrid Memory Buffers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12624)
*Jiuming Liu,Mengmeng Liu,Guangming Wang,Lihao Liu,Michael Ying Yang,Yunpeng Zhang,Francesco Nex,Hao Cheng,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MemReg，一种带位姿相关记忆缓冲的多帧室外LiDAR点云配准网络，突破成对输入范式，在KITTI、nuScenes、Apollo-Southbay上取得SOTA，并可泛化至室内多视图场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室外LiDAR配准工作受限于成对输入范式，忽略了流式序列中的时序相关性，难以利用长期时序上下文完成稀疏补全、离群点滤除及低重叠问题处理，从而制约配准性能。

Method: 设计多帧室外点云配准网络并引入位姿相关记忆缓冲：包含隐式记忆特征缓冲与显式记忆位姿缓冲，用于存储并动态更新位姿相关时序特征；同时提出动态历史加权模块，自适应融合当前与历史位姿相关特征。

Result: 在KITTI、nuScenes、Apollo-Southbay三个室外数据集上达到SOTA，超越所有基于成对的方法及多帧SLAM系统；在3DMatch、3DLoMatch、ScanNet等室内多视图配准任务上也展现出较强泛化性与竞争力。

Conclusion: 长期时序LiDAR序列可提供丰富全局上下文信息，借助混合记忆缓冲与动态加权融合机制可显著提升配准性能，验证了多帧时序建模在配准任务中的有效性与跨域泛化能力。代码将开源。

Abstract: Prior outdoor LiDAR registration works are commonly limited by the pair-wise input paradigm, which neglects temporal correlations inherent within streaming sequences. In this paper, we propose a novel multi-frame outdoor point cloud registration network for streaming LiDAR scans enhanced with pose-related memory buffers. The key observation is that long-term temporal LiDAR sequences can provide rich global contextual information to complete sparse measurements, filter outliers, and address low-overlap problems, thereby boosting registration performance. Specifically, two hybrid memory buffers are designed, including an implicit memory feature buffer and an explicit memory pose buffer, to store and dynamically update pose-related temporal features. Moreover, a novel dynamic history weighting module is developed to adaptively fuse current and history pose-related features. Extensive experiments on three outdoor datasets, including KITTI, nuScenes, and Apollo-Southbay, demonstrate state-of-the-art performance of MemReg, surpassing all previous pair-wise methods and also multi-frame SLAM systems. Our method also generalizes surprisingly well to multiview indoor registration scenarios with rather competitive performance on 3DMatch, 3DLoMatch, and ScanNet. Code will be released at https://github.com/IRMVLab/MemReg.

</details>


### [10] [ForcingBooster: Adaptive Patch-wise Denoising for Fast Autoregressive Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12625)
*Zekai Gu,Peng Wang,Jiahao Lu,Yongsen Mao,Cheng Lin,Yuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出动态按patch分配去噪步数的框架，易patch早停、难patch多细化，在四步自回归视频扩散基线上减少约50%去噪步数且视觉质量相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归视频扩散模型在生成下一视频块时对所有patch均匀分配计算，采用相同去噪调度和函数评估次数，忽视了视频内容的空间异质性。静态、简单或重复区域更易去噪，而其他区域需要更多细化或上下文。均匀预算导致简单patch冗余计算，而简单减少固定步数又会使困难patch细化不足。

Method: 提出一种通用框架，动态为单个patch分配去噪步数：容易的patch提前停止以提升效率，困难的patch获得更多细化步数。该方法兼容大多数现有少步自回归扩散模型，仅需一个轻量级路由模块与少量训练。

Result: 在视频patch上应用的总去噪步数减少约50%，同时保持与四步基线相当的视觉质量，生成加速且质量退化可忽略。

Conclusion: 通过空间异质性驱动的动态去噪步数分配，可在几乎不损失质量的前提下显著加速自回归视频扩散生成，且具备良好的通用性与兼容性。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models have become a leading approach to real-time interactive video generation and video world modeling. However, existing methods typically allocate computation uniformly across space when generating the next video chunk, updating all patches with the same denoising schedule and number of function evaluations. This ignores the spatial heterogeneity of video content: static, simple, or recurring regions are often easier to denoise, while other regions require further refinement or additional context. A uniform budget thus leads to redundant computation on easy patches, whereas reducing the fixed step count can leave difficult patches insufficiently refined. We introduce \methodname{}, a general framework that dynamically allocates denoising steps to individual patches: easy patches stop early to improve efficiency, while difficult patches receive more refinement steps. This design accelerates generation with negligible quality degradation. Our method is compatible with most existing few-step autoregressive diffusion models, requiring only a lightweight routing module with modest training. Experiments demonstrate that \methodname{} reduces the total number of denoising steps applied across video patches by about 50\% while maintaining visual quality comparable to the four-step baseline.

</details>


### [11] [Feature Competition and Its Representational Origins in Vision Language Models: Evidence from the Stroop Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12650)
*Nurbüke Teker,Rui Xiao,Zeynep Akata,Shuchen Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究将心理学经典Stroop范式引入视觉语言模型（VLM）评测，发现当图像中的文字与颜色相互冲突时，模型普遍存在偏向文字特征的持久偏差；在表征层面，文字线索比颜色线索表征得更强、更可分离，这种表征不对称性可预测干预（steering）的难易程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM依据图像回答问题，但回答本质上是一种"选择"：当同一图像中存在多个与问题同等相关的特征（如文字与颜色）时，模型如何取舍尚不清楚。作者借用心理学中研究特征冲突选择的经典Stroop任务，来系统考察VLM在多重竞争性线索下的行为倾向及其内部成因。

Method: 将Stroop任务改编为基准测试，构造文字与颜色相互冲突的图像（如用蓝色书写的"RED"），要求模型报告颜色；对多种对比式（contrastive）与生成式（generative）VLM进行行为评测。随后打开黑箱，在嵌入空间中分析文字与颜色线索的表征强度与可分离性，并进行表征干预（steering）实验以检验其对行为的影响。

Result: 各模型均表现出对文字特征的持续偏差：当文字与颜色不一致时，模型优先给出以文字为中心的答案。在嵌入空间中，文字线索的表示比颜色线索更强且更可分离；这种表征不对称性可预测干预效果——将模型引导至文字相关方向显著易于引导至颜色相关方向。

Conclusion: 借助经典心理学范式，研究在行为与表征两个层面证明：同一图像内的不同特征对VLM行为的影响并不均等。VLM会对同一图像中的不同特征赋予不同显著度（saliency），这提示需要理解线索特异性显著度如何塑造模型在主流下游任务中的表现。

Abstract: When you ask a vision-language model (VLM) a question with an image, it answers your question based on the image. But to act means to choose: saying one thing means choosing to respond not say another. This becomes an issue when the image contains multiple features that equally relate to the question at hand: how would VLMs choose to respond? This problem is precisely studied by the classical Stroop task in psychology: among a set of deliberately designed images whose text and color conflicts (e.g., \textcolor{blue}{RED}), participants are instructed to report the color. We adapt this psychological paradigm to a benchmark and prompted a range of contrastive and generative VLMs on their behavior. Across models, we observe a persistent bias toward the feature of text in Stroop images: when text and color disagree, models preferentially generate responses centered to the text rather than color. We then opened the black box and examined the source of this bias and why certain features influences the model's behavior more than others. In the embedding space, we found that textual cues are represented more strongly and separably than color cues. This representational asymmetry predicts intervention success: steering models toward text-related directions is substantially easier than steering them toward color-related directions. Using a classical psychological paradigm, we demonstrate, at both behavioral and representational levels, that distinct features within the same image can exert unequal influence on VLM behavior. Our findings suggest that VLMs assign different degrees of saliency to different features within the same image, highlighting the need to understand how cue-specific saliency shapes their performance in widely used downstream applications.

</details>


### [12] [WoodBR-Cross: A public wood-image dataset and individual-tree-disjoint benchmark for species identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12676)
*Daniel Casanova,Alexandre Bahia Gontijo,Arnaldo Candido Junior,Silvana Nisgoski,Pedro Luiz de Paula Filho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出WoodBR-Cross木材横切面图像基准，包含4,875张图像、38个分类单元、303棵经巴西林务局馆藏编号（codigos-testemunho）唯一标识的树木，并设计三项任务（A、B1、B2）对比重复感知与单树不相交划分。B2确保同一棵树的所有图像不跨训练/验证/测试集。在ResNet-50、ConvNeXt-Tiny、ViT-B/16及手工特征、冻结特征与对照实验中，验证集选择的ConvNeXt-Tiny在B2上取得树级macro-F1为0.8621 ± 0.0317，冻结DINOv2特征达0.9000 ± 0.0722，用于估计对未见树木的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有木材图像基准在训练集与测试集中同时出现同一棵树的图像时，会产生过于乐观的性能估计，无法反映模型对未见树木的真实泛化能力。因此需要一个以单棵树为划分单位、避免数据泄漏的基准来提供更可靠的性能评估。

Method: 构建WoodBR-Cross数据集，含4,875张横切面图像、38个操作分类单元、303棵经馆藏编号唯一标识的树木；物种级子集含3,406张图像、20类、214棵树。设计三项任务：A为全部类别的重复感知图像划分，B1为物种子集的图像划分，B2为该子集的单树不相交划分（同一棵树的所有图像保持在一折内，训练/验证/测试不共享树木）。评估ResNet-50、ConvNeXt-Tiny、ViT-B/16，采用三折外层交叉验证、内部验证与五个训练随机种子，并加入手工特征、冻结特征基线以及仅边界和随机标签对照。

Result: 验证集选择的ConvNeXt-Tiny在B2任务上取得树级macro-F1为0.8621 ± 0.0317；冻结DINOv2特征取得0.9000 ± 0.0722。数值为三个种子平均折均值上的均值与标准差。

Conclusion: WoodBR-Cross通过单树不相交的划分方式，提供了对来自该馆藏的未见树木性能的可靠估计，避免了同树图像跨集导致的乐观偏差，为木材图像识别研究建立了更严格的评估基准。

Abstract: Wood-image benchmarks can yield optimistic estimates when images from the same tree occur in both training and test sets. WoodBR-Cross contains 4,875 transverse-surface images in 38 operational taxonomic classes, linked to 303 distinct trees identified by numeric catalogue witness codes (codigos-testemunho) in the Brazilian Forest Service collection. A species-level subset comprises 3,406 images, 20 classes and 214 trees. Three tasks compare duplicate-aware image partitions for all classes (A), image partitions for the species subset (B1), and individual-tree-disjoint partitions for that subset (B2). In B2, all images from each tree are kept together, so no tree is shared across training, validation or test within a fold. ResNet-50, ConvNeXt-Tiny and ViT-B/16 were evaluated with three outer folds, internal validation and five training seeds. Handcrafted and frozen-feature baselines complement border-only and randomized-label controls. Validation-selected ConvNeXt-Tiny achieved B2 tree-level macro-F1 of 0.8621 +/- 0.0317; frozen DINOv2 features yielded 0.9000 +/- 0.0722. Values are means and standard deviations across three seed-averaged fold means. Results estimate performance on previously unseen trees from this collection.

</details>


### [13] [Bridging Human and Model Attention: Explainability Analysis of CNN, Mamba, and ViT Architectures with Gaze-Based Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12703)
*Marco Stefani,Edoardo Parpaiola,Matteo Bino,Gianluca Caregnato,Francesco Roncolato,Daniel Fusaro,Loris Nanni,Alberto Pretto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对CNN、ViT与ViM进行系统可解释性评估，用眼动数据量化模型注意力与人类视觉感知的对齐，并提出标准化归因-注视概率映射流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险深度学习应用需要精确刻画模型决策过程，而现有研究多为定性分析，缺乏对模型注意力与人类视觉感知一致性的定量评估。

Method: 构建标准化归一化流程，将事后归因图与眼动注视热图映射为空间概率分布；在细粒度分类、医学影像和情绪识别任务上，使用各架构专用XAI方法，以Jensen-Shannon相似度、卡方相似度和Pearson相关性三个互补指标度量保真度，并分析骨干冻结与集成策略的影响。

Result: CNN注意力更全局、显著图更弥散，在一般场景下与人类感知相关性较好；ViT呈细粒度patch级注意力，聚焦判别性微特征；ViM存在偏置行为，在医学任务中可解释性强，但在自然图像中强度分布发散，出现支持重叠接近1而线性相关性消失的分离现象。

Conclusion: 架构与训练策略选择显著影响模型显著性与人类视觉注意力的对齐程度，所提度量体系能够显式刻画不同架构的注意力动态差异。

Abstract: High-risk applications of deep learning require precise characterization of model decision-making. We conduct a systematic explainability evaluation across Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs), Vision Transformers (ViTs), and Vision Mamba (ViM). Unlike predominantly qualitative studies, we quantify the alignment between model attention and human visual perception using eye-tracking ground truth. We formalize this comparison via a standardized normalization pipeline mapping post-hoc attribution maps and gaze heatmaps onto spatial probability distributions. Alignment is evaluated across fine-grained classification, medical imaging, and emotion recognition using architecture-specific XAI methods. We measure fidelity using Jensen-Shannon similarity, Chi-square similarity, and Pearson correlation, three complementary estimators capturing support overlap, mass discrepancy, and structural co-variation. Our results reveal distinct attention dynamics: CNNs tend to exhibit a more global focus, with spatially more diffuse saliency maps that correlate well with human perception in general contexts, while ViTs demonstrate fine-grained, patch-level attention, often focused on discriminative microfeatures. The Mamba architecture shows biased behavior: it achieves strong explainability in medical tasks but exhibits divergent intensity distributions in natural images; our analytical characterization of the metrics makes this dissociation explicit, since near-unit support overlap is attained simultaneously with a vanishing linear correlation. We further analyze the effect of backbone freezing and ensemble strategies on model interpretability. The results highlight how architectural and training choices influence the alignment between model saliency and human visual attention.

</details>


### [14] [Zebra3D: Efficient Hybrid Foundation Models For Scalable 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12727)
*Utkarsh Saxena,Ruijing Yang,Mehdi Rezagholizadeh,Akash Haridas,Vikram Appia,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Zebra3D：面向前馈3D基础模型的逐层稀疏-线性混合注意力框架，免从头训练即可提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈3D基础模型依赖全局注意力重建无位姿图像场景，但二次复杂度限制其处理大规模视图集的可扩展性。

Method: 观测到深度方向上一致的注意力模式：早期层对线性替换容忍度高，中后期敏感层注意力更集中。据此为容忍层分配线性中心注意力、敏感层分配分块稀疏注意力，并用landmark摘要保留未选中上下文；闭式仿射拟合实现免训练转换，可选转换后训练结合任务监督与输出、关系特征蒸馏。

Result: 在VGGT、HunyuanWorld-Mirror、π³三个骨干上，高注意力稀疏度下重建与相机位姿精度良好，免训练变体接近稠密版本；端到端加速随视图数增加，1024视图达7.1倍；转换后训练的Zebra3D-VGGT在94%平均注意力稀疏度下达到80.5% RealEstate10K AUC@30，与稠密VGGT微调相差1.1个百分点。

Conclusion: Zebra3D通过逐层混合注意力在免从头训练前提下显著提升3D基础模型对大规模视图的可扩展性与效率，同时保持接近稠密模型的精度。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D foundation models reconstruct scenes from unposed images, but the quadratic cost of global attention limits their scalability to large view sets. We present Zebra3D, a layerwise sparse-linear hybrid attention framework that improves the efficiency of pretrained 3D foundation models without training from scratch. Across VGGT, HunyuanWorld-Mirror, and $π^3$, we observe a broadly consistent depth-wise pattern: early stages are relatively tolerant to linear replacement, while sensitive middle-to-late stages tend to exhibit more concentrated attention. Zebra3D exploits this structure by assigning linear-centered attention to tolerant stages and tile-sparse attention to sensitive stages, using landmark summaries to preserve unselected context. Closed-form affine fitting enables training-free conversion, while optional post-conversion training combines task supervision with output and relational feature distillation. Across all three backbones, Zebra3D achieves strong reconstruction and camera-pose accuracy at high attention sparsity, with training-free variants remaining close to their dense counterparts. End-to-end speedups increase with input view count, reaching up to $7.1\times$ at 1,024 views. After post-conversion training, Zebra3D-VGGT reaches 80.5\% RealEstate10K AUC@30 at 94\% mean attention sparsity, within 1.1 percentage points of dense VGGT fine-tuned on the same data and schedule.

</details>


### [15] [Reference-Free Food Volume Estimation from Egocentric Stereo Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12735)
*Vivan Kumar,Gautham Vinod,Fengqing Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出利用可穿戴设备立体视频，通过FoundationStereo、SAM 2与TSDF融合实现全自动真实尺度食物三维重建与体积估计，五个食物平均绝对百分比误差9.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于立体视觉的食物份量估计依赖用户用手机拍摄多张图像或在场景中放置参照物进行尺度标定，使用负担重，难以支持被动的膳食摄入监测。

Method: 使用可穿戴第一视角设备的立体视频，利用FoundationStereo进行零样本立体匹配，SAM 2进行食物分割，对一组关键帧做TSDF融合，重建出真实物理尺度的餐盘食物三维网格；借助可穿戴设备的出厂标定立体基线与设备端VIO估计的相机位姿，无需场景内尺度参照物即可全自动重建。

Result: 在录制的五种食物上取得较强初步体积估计结果，平均绝对百分比误差为9.1%。

Conclusion: 该方法无需参照物与多视角拍摄，可从单次立体录像自动生成视觉与度量均准确的食物三维网格，证明可穿戴立体视觉用于被动膳食监测中真实尺度体积估计的可行性。

Abstract: Current stereo vision-based approaches for food portion estimation are hindered by the user burden of using a mobile phone to capture multiple images or placing a reference object in the scene for scale calibration. As a step towards passive dietary intake monitoring, we introduce a method that uses stereo video from a wearable egocentric device to reconstruct food items in true physical scale to accurately estimate volume. By utilizing FoundationStereo for zero-shot stereo matching, SAM 2 for food segmentation, and TSDF fusion over a set of keyframes, our method yields a visually and metrically accurate 3D mesh of any plated food item from a single stereo recording. We leverage the unique offerings of wearables, such as a factory-calibrated stereo baseline and camera poses estimated on-device via visual-inertial odometry (VIO), to perform fully automatic reconstruction without a scale reference object in the scene. We show strong initial volume estimation results across five recorded food items, achieving a mean absolute percentage error of 9.1%.

</details>


### [16] [Shift-Aware Identity-Guided Latent Refinement for Referring Audio--Visual Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12783)
*Kun Li,Sami Sebastian Brandt,Michael Ying Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SAIL框架，通过双令牌解耦与移位感知细化，解决参考音视频分割中目标漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ref-AVS需融合视觉、音频与语言三模态进行细粒度定位分割，但现有方法在瞬时遮挡、声音衰减或身份模糊引发的目标漂移下难以保持一致性。

Method: SAIL移位感知身份引导潜变量细化框架：采用双令牌策略将帧级动态与全局语义锚点解耦，显式建模目标移位线索；设计移位感知细化模块，通过对齐令牌与领域特定证据纠正漂移；解码器将细化嵌入与连续记忆更新结合，维持时空连贯与身份一致性。

Result: 在两个Ref-AVS基准上实验，显著优于现有AVS、Ref-VOS及Ref-AVS方法。

Conclusion: SAIL有效提升Ref-AVS在目标漂移场景下的分割鲁棒性与身份一致性，代码将于发表后公开。

Abstract: Referring Audio--Visual Segmentation (Ref-AVS) is an emergent multimodal task requiring the precise identification and segmentation of a specific object based on a natural language expression grounded in both visual and auditory cues. Unlike traditional AVS, which segments generic sound sources, or referring video object segmentation (Ref-VOS), which relies solely on visual--textual grounding, Ref-AVS necessitates fine-grained tri-modal interaction across vision, audio, and language. However, existing methods often fail to maintain consistency when subjected to target shift, a phenomenon where the model erroneously transfers its focus between objects due to transient occlusions, acoustic fading, or identity ambiguities. To address this, we propose a novel Shift-Aware Identity-guided Latent (SAIL) refinement framework. It introduces a dual-token strategy that decouples frame-level dynamics from a global semantic anchor to explicitly model target-shift cues. We design a shift-aware refinement module that rectifies drift by aligning tokens with domain-specific evidence. Finally, we leverage a decoder that integrates the refined embeddings with a continuous memory update to maintain spatio-temporal coherence and identity consistency throughout the video sequence. Extensive experiments conducted on two Ref-AVS benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed method, significantly outperforming existing AVS, Ref-VOS, and Ref-AVS approaches. The code will be released after publication.

</details>


### [17] [Domain Adaptation of SAM 2.1 for Building-Component Segmentation in Image-Based 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12786)
*Ehsan Agha Ebrahimi,Yong-Cheol Lee,Keith G. Mills*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究基于HBD数据集微调SAM 2.1，提升建筑图像多类语义分割性能（mIoU从49.9%提升至69.6%），并将微调后的掩码用于神经辐射场（GARField）中的3D分组，以支持建筑构件的3D自动重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于图像的3D建筑构件重建依赖于2D建筑图像的精确分割，而将2D掩码在3D环境中进行一致分组需要更强的分割模型；现有方法（如GARField中使用的SAM）在建筑多类分割上表现不足，因此需要针对建筑构件进行领域适配。

Method: 在HBD数据集（42栋建筑的2,679张标注图像，覆盖11类构件：墙、窗、天花板、门、柱、梁、地板、洞口、幕墙、屋顶、电梯）上微调SAM 2.1；采用逐类IoU、Dice和mIoU进行评价；以SegFormer作为基线对比；随后在神经辐射场中对建筑图像掩码进行分组，并在新渲染图像中比较预训练与微调SAM 2.1的分组效果。

Result: mIoU由49.9%提升至69.6%；与SegFormer基线相比，SAM 2.1在部分表面积较大的对象类别上表现更优；在神经辐射场中，微调后的SAM 2.1在用户选定的分组尺度下对建筑构件的分组效果优于预训练版本。

Conclusion: 微调SAM 2.1显著改善了建筑构件的2D多类分割与3D分组质量，推动了GARField在3D中的应用，为建筑结构的3D自动重建提供了更准确的分割支持。

Abstract: 3D reconstruction of building components using image-based scanning requires appropriate segmentation of the objects in the 2D building images. In order to benefit from consistent grouping of the 2D masks in 3D environments, vision foundation models such as the Segment Anything Model have been utilized by Group Anything with Radiance Fields (GARField) to perform 3D segmentation. This study fine-tuned SAM 2.1 on the HBD dataset, consisting of 2,679 annotated images from 42 buildings, to enhance multi-class segmentation of 2D building images, covering 11 classes (Wall, Window, Ceiling, Door, Column, Beam, Floor, Opening, Curtain Wall, Roof, and Lift). The model was evaluated using per-class IoU, Dice, and mIoU metrics, and the results show an improvement in mIoU from 49.9% to 69.6%, indicating a meaningful improvement in class segmentation and providing a more accurate model for distilling the masks in 3D. SegFormer was then used as a baseline, generating per-class masks for comparison with SAM 2.1, which indicates improvement in some object classes with larger surface area. The masks in the building images were grouped in the neural radiance field, and the pretrained SAM 2.1 was compared with the fine-tuned SAM 2.1 in the newly rendered images. This comparison shows that fine-tuning improves the grouping of building components at a user-selected grouping scale. This advances the application of Group Anything with Radiance Fields (GARField) in 3D, enabling better segmentation of building structures for 3D auto-reconstruction.

</details>


### [18] [Spend Where It Matters: Particle Search with MH Refinement for Budget-Constrained Audio-Visual Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12811)
*Shivanshu Shekhar,Raghavendra Addanki,Tong Zhang,Uttaran Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无需训练、与奖励无关的两阶段框架 Spend Where It Matters（SWIM）：先用自适应粒子搜索定位优质候选，再用 Metropolis-Hastings 局部精修，在固定算力预算下提升联合音视频生成的对齐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励引导的推理时对齐可提升联合音视频生成质量，但多目标优化需大量昂贵的模型评估；粒子搜索虽能全局探索，却受有限种群导致估计误差的限制；MH 精修擅长局部改进，但依赖良好初始化。因此需要在有限算力下兼顾全局探索与局部精修。

Method: 两阶段、免训练、奖励无关框架。第一阶段基于在线方差估计，在去噪检查点间自适应分配粒子，最小化估计方差并将算力集中于有用处，从而得到优质候选；第二阶段以该候选为起点，在完整输出上使用"加噪—去噪"局部提议进行 MH 精修，无需更大粒子群或昂贵全局探索。

Result: 在匹配算力下，粒子搜索与 MH 精修结合优于任一单独方法。在 JavisBench-Mini 上，借助 JavisDiT++ 时七项指标平均相对提升 30.7%（对比 BoN）与 14.1%（对比 EvoSearch）；借助 LTX-2 时提升 9.2%（对比 BoN）与 17.0%（对比 EvoSearch）。在 SD1.5 图像生成上，自适应粒子分配较均匀分配将 AestheticScore 提升 2.2%、ImageReward 提升 6.0%。

Conclusion: 自适应粒子分配与 MH 局部精修的两阶段组合，能在固定算力预算下有效提升奖励引导的联合音视频生成质量，并可推广至图像生成任务。

Abstract: Reward-guided inference-time alignment can improve joint audio-visual generation, but optimizing multiple alignment objectives requires many expensive model evaluations, making existing methods costly under limited compute. Particle-based search enables global exploration but incurs estimation error with a finite population, while Metropolis-Hastings (MH) can refine candidates locally but benefits from a strong initialization. We introduce \textbf{S}pend \textbf{W}here \textbf{I}t \textbf{M}atters (\modelname), a two-stage, training-free, reward-agnostic framework that combines particle search with MH refinement under a fixed compute budget. In the first stage, we adaptively allocate particles across denoising checkpoints using online variance estimates, minimizing estimation variance while concentrating compute where it is most useful. This search provides a promising candidate for MH refinement, which uses local noise-and-denoise proposals on completed outputs to further improve reward. By starting from a strong candidate, MH can recover additional reward without requiring a larger particle population or costly global exploration. We show that, at matched compute, combining particle search with MH refinement achieves higher rewards than either approach alone. On \textsc{JavisBench-Mini}, at closely matched compute, \modelname achieves mean relative gains across seven metrics of $30.7\%$ over BoN and $14.1\%$ over EvoSearch with JavisDiT++, and $9.2\%$ over BoN and $17.0\%$ over EvoSearch with LTX-2. Beyond joint audio-visual generation, we also demonstrate the benefits of adaptive particle allocation on SD1.5 image generation, improving AestheticScore by $2.2\%$ and ImageReward by $6.0\%$ over uniform allocation.

</details>


### [19] [Structure-Aware Matching for Robust UAV Thermal-to-Satellite Geo-Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12835)
*Chun Liu,Yuxin Wang,Zhenxiang Huo,Linlin Zhu,Tao Xu,Henggang Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出结构感知匹配框架SAMF，利用热红外与卫星图像间稳定的跨模态结构线索，提升无人机热红外地理定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机热红外-卫星地理定位可在GNSS拒止和低能见度条件下实现自主导航，但热红外图与卫星图在颜色、亮度和视角上存在显著跨模态差异，导致传统基于外观的方法难以建立可靠对应关系；现有方法多关注全局模态偏移，忽略了两种视图间固有的不变场景结构。

Method: 提出SAMF结构感知匹配框架，显式利用稳定的跨模态结构线索。其中方向结构增强模块（DSEM）通过捕获方向性和低频空间模式来强化鲁棒的结构表征；模态感知仿射重映射模块（MARM）随后对增强特征进行仿射校正，进一步促进跨模态对齐。此外，基于Thermal-UAV数据集构建了Thermal-UAV-Paired基准。

Result: 在Boson-nighttime和Thermal-UAV-Paired两个数据集上的大量实验表明，SAMF达到了当前最优性能；消融实验验证了DSEM与MARM的有效性及其协同互补性。代码与数据集已开源。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模并利用跨模态稳定结构线索，SAMF有效缓解了热红外与卫星图像间的模态差异，为无人机热红外地理定位提供了高性能方案，并公开了代码与基准数据集以促进后续研究。

Abstract: UAV thermal-to-satellite geo-localization localizes UAVs by matching real-time thermal images against georeferenced satellite images, enabling autonomous navigation in GNSS-denied and low-visibility conditions. However, substantial cross-modal disparities in color, brightness and viewpoint hinder conventional appearance-based methods from establishing reliable correspondences. While existing approaches predominantly address global modality shifts, they overlook the invariant scene structures inherently shared between thermal and satellite views. To bridge this gap, we propose SAMF, a Structure-Aware Matching Framework that explicitly exploits stable cross-modal structural cues to improve UAV thermal geolocalization. Specifically, the Directional Structure Enhancement Module (DSEM) strengthens robust structural representations by capturing directional and low-frequency spatial patterns, and the Modality-Aware Affine Remapping Module (MARM) subsequently applies affine correction to these enhanced features, further promoting cross-modal alignment. Additionally, we introduce the Thermal-UAV-Paired benchmark derived from the Thermal-UAV dataset. Extensive experiments on both the Boson-nighttime and Thermal-UAV-Paired datasets show that SAMF achieves state-of-the-art performance. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness and synergistic complementarity of DSEM and MARM. The code and dataset are released at: https://github.com/YuxinWang879/SAMF.git.

</details>


### [20] [APEX: Asynchronous Prefix Denoising for Extreme Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12843)
*David Dinkevich,Nisan Chiprut,Yoav HaCohen,Dani Lischinski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: APEX提出一种在极度隐空间压缩下兼顾重建与生成的扩散方法：通过分层通道前缀（结构到细节）与异步通道组去噪，在ImageNet-512的64x/128x压缩下超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 更少的隐token意味着更低的扩散成本，但激进的隐压缩会造成"重建-生成"困境：增加通道数能提升重建质量，却使生成变得更加困难。如何在极端压缩下同时保证重建与生成质量是核心问题。

Method: APEX（Asynchronous Prefix denoising for EXtreme compression）：将分层隐空间与跨通道组的异步去噪相结合。其自编码器把通道前缀按从结构到细节的顺序排列，由一个共享去噪器在不增加token数的前提下，以错开的局部时间对不同通道组进行生成。

Result: 在ImageNet-512上，APEX在64x与128x压缩下所有评测通道数上均优于现有方法；在同等训练与引导采样计算量下，于各有评测预算下均超越256-token模型，且在计算量越低时增益越大。

Conclusion: 通过通道前缀分层与异步去噪，APEX在不增加token的前提下缓解了极端隐压缩的生成-重建权衡，在低计算预算下展现出更明显的优势。

Abstract: Fewer latent tokens promise cheaper diffusion. Yet aggressive latent compression creates a reconstruction-generation dilemma: adding channels improves reconstruction but makes generation harder. We introduce APEX, short for Asynchronous Prefix denoising for EXtreme compression, which pairs a hierarchical latent space with asynchronous denoising over channel groups. Our autoencoder orders channel prefixes from structure to detail, while one shared denoiser generates them at staggered local times without adding tokens. On ImageNet-512, APEX outperforms existing methods at every evaluated channel count under 64x and 128x compression. At matched training and guided sampling compute, it also outperforms a 256-token model at every evaluated budget, with larger gains at lower compute. Code is available at https://daviddinkevich.github.io/APEX.

</details>


### [21] [CaptchaAgent: Verifiable Visual Reasoning for CAPTCHA Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12866)
*Shangquan Sun,Ruoyu Chen,Zhirui Liu,Xiang Liu,Chenghao Qian,Wenqi Ren,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出结构化视觉推理框架 CaptchaAgent，通过视觉中间表示、符号化 ASCII 协议、局部证据精修与反事实一致性验证，显著提升交互式验证码求解的 Pass@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推理型验证码要求细粒度感知、组合关系推理与基于浏览器交互的落地执行，而端到端视觉语言模型智能体在此场景中较为脆弱：漏检目标、计数错误或关系混淆会直接传播到最终动作，导致失败。

Method: 不再直接从截图预测动作，而是先构建视觉中间表示（V-IR），显式记录实体、属性及空间/拓扑关系；采用仅含符号的 ASCII 协议，强制解析器提交确定的对象集合与计数；在此基础上进行局部证据精修，以更高有效分辨率重访歧义区域；并通过反事实一致性验证，检验所得动作在保持任务语义的变换下是否稳定。

Result: 在 Open CaptchaWorld 上，使用三个专有骨干模型时，较商用浏览器智能体基线在相同骨干下 Pass@1 提升 21.3–25.8 个百分点；在离线 Oedipus-data 基准上，三个匹配骨干下平均 Pass@1 较 Oedipus 求解器提升 9.2–12.3 个百分点。中间轨迹显示，精确计数、连续几何与有序执行仍是自动化难题的主要来源。

Conclusion: 结构化中间表示与显式承诺机制可有效缓解端到端智能体的误差传播，提升组合式验证码求解性能；同时为设计更具鲁棒性的组合型验证码提供实践指导。

Abstract: Modern reasoning CAPTCHAs require fine-grained perception, compositional relation reasoning, and grounded browser interaction. End-to-end vision-language model agents remain brittle in this setting because a missed object, incorrect count, or confused relation can propagate directly to the final action. To address this problem, we present CaptchaAgent, a structured visual reasoning framework for interactive CAPTCHA solving. Instead of predicting an action directly from a screenshot, CaptchaAgent first constructs a visual intermediate representation (V-IR) that explicitly records entities, attributes, and spatial or topological relations. A symbol-only ASCII protocol further requires the parser to commit to a concrete object set and count. Building on this global representation, localized evidence refinement revisits ambiguous regions at higher effective resolution, while counterfactual consistency verification tests whether the resulting action remains stable under task-preserving transformations. Across three proprietary backbones on Open CaptchaWorld, CaptchaAgent improves Pass@1 by 21.3-25.8 percentage points over a commercially available browser agent baseline evaluated with the same backbones. On the separate offline Oedipus-data benchmark, it further improves average Pass@1 by 9.2-12.3 percentage points over the Oedipus solver across three matched backbones. The intermediate traces show that exact counting, continuous geometry, and ordered execution remain major sources of automation difficulty for the evaluated agents, providing practical guidance for designing more resilient compositional CAPTCHAs.

</details>


### [22] [Selective Spatio-Temporal Fruit Inspector (S$^2$FI): A Mamba-based Framework for Rotational Fruit Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12868)
*Hao Tian,Jie Hu,Guoqi Shan,Fan Wang,Wenzhuo Zhang,Youshan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出低成本轻量化水果分拣系统S$^2$FI，结合3D卷积编码器与Mamba骨干网络提取时空特征，配合双传送带与弧形轨道硬件设计，在自建6,460样本数据集上实现准确率、效率与实用性的最佳权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球果蔬产业中，中小规模种植者面临严重的采后分拣难题：大型智能分拣系统成本过高，而人工分拣效率低、标准不一致、劳动力需求大。研究旨在填补昂贵设备与低效人工之间的空白，为中小种植者提供低成本、高效率、轻量化的深度学习水果分拣方案。

Method: 提出Selective Spatio-Temporal Fruit Inspector（S$^2$FI）架构，将3D卷积编码器与基于Mamba的骨干网络相结合，高效提取时空特征，在水果旋转过程中自适应加权信息量大的帧，并以线性复杂度建模长程依赖，从而减轻运动模糊与遮挡影响。硬件采用双传送带与弧形轨道设计，支持家庭供电、快速部署与轻量化嵌入式部署以提升经济性。同时构建了基于传送带的水果质量视频数据集，含6,460个样本。

Result: 在留出测试集上，该方法在准确率、效率与实用性之间取得最佳权衡，性能优于所对比的基线方法，实现了面向实际分拣场景的准确率、速度与实用性的良好平衡。

Conclusion: S$^2$FI系统通过软硬件协同设计，有效解决了中小种植者采后分拣的成本与效率痛点，为低成本、轻量化水果智能分拣提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The global fruit and vegetable industry confronts severe post-harvest sorting challenges for small and medium-scale growers. Large intelligent sorting systems are too costly, while manual sorting suffers from low efficiency, inconsistent standards, and high labor demand. To bridge this gap between expensive equipment and inefficient manual work, this study develops a low-cost, high-efficiency, lightweight fruit sorting system driven by deep learning, suitable for small and medium growers. We propose the Selective Spatio-Temporal Fruit Inspector (S$^2$FI) architecture, which combines a 3D convolutional encoder with a Mamba-based backbone.It efficiently extracts spatiotemporal features, adaptively weighs informative frames during fruit rotation, and models long-range dependencies with linear complexity, reducing motion blur and occlusion. The hardware uses a dual-conveyor and curved rail design, supporting household power, fast deployment, and lightweight embedded deployment to improve affordability. We also built a conveyor-based fruit quality video dataset with 6,460 samples. Experiments on the held-out test set show that our method achieves the best trade-off among accuracy, efficiency, and practicality, outperforming the compared baselines on the test set, achieving an excellent balance of accuracy, speed, and practicality for real-world sorting.

</details>


### [23] [ROSETTA: Rotation Subspace Estimation for Training-free Transmodal Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12871)
*Alex Junho Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练校准层ROSETTA，通过在白化特征空间中估计局部旋转实现异构编码器对齐，在多种跨模态/设备/表征/骨干设置中以毫秒级开销验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同传感器或编码器独立训练，会给特征空间施加各自不同的坐标系，且不存在统一的规范参考系，也没有单一训练流程能完全弥合这一差距，因此跨编码器匹配存在对齐难题。

Method: 作者首先从理论上刻画：任意两个此类系统间的相对变换是白化特征空间中的保距旋转，其角速度呈白噪声特性（范数大、时间不相关、无法从特征本身预测）；但编码器输出间的互协方差具有低有效秩，仅约束少量旋转参数，因而局部对齐可行。基于此提出ROSETTA：一个免训练的校准层，通过期望最大化（EM）估计局部旋转，查询阶段无需共同观测。

Result: 在三个公开驾驶数据集上验证，覆盖LiDAR、相机、热成像与事件模态，涵盖跨模态、跨设备、跨表征与跨骨干等设置，每个查询开销为毫秒级。

Conclusion: ROSETTA表明异构编码器的特征空间对齐可通过免训练的局部旋转估计实现，兼具理论依据与实用效率。

Abstract: When a query must be matched against a database built with a different sensor or processed by a different encoder, the independently trained encoders impose distinct coordinate systems on the feature space. No canonical reference frame exists to which all encoders can be aligned, and no single training procedure has fully closed this gap. We show that the relative transformation between any two such systems is a spatially preserving rotation in whitened feature space, whose angular velocity exhibits white-noise characteristics: large in norm, temporally uncorrelated, and unpredictable from the features themselves. However, the cross-covariance between encoder outputs has low effective rank, constraining only a small fraction of the rotation parameters, therefore local alignment is tractable. ROSETTA is a training-free calibration layer that estimates the local rotation via an expectation-maximization procedure, requiring no coobservation at query time. We validate ROSETTA on three public driving datasets spanning LiDAR, camera, thermal, and event modalities in cross-modal, cross-device, cross-representation, and cross-backbone settings, with milliseconds overhead per query.

</details>


### [24] [Win-Win Fusion: Multi-Modal Image Fusion with Visual-Semantic Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12887)
*Hao Zhang,Xuhui Zuo,Jiayi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出W2F，一种面向多模态图像融合的视觉-语义互增强范式，通过分割引导融合损失、信息恢复先验、多模态交互注意力与协同耦合机制，缩小视觉与语义域的优化鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态图像融合方法在视觉域与语义域之间存在优化鸿沟，限制了感知能力的提升，因此需要一种能同时强化视觉与语义表现的互增强框架。

Method: 1) 强化视觉-语义协作基础：引入分割引导融合损失，将目标感知结构引导注入视觉融合；引入嵌入式信息恢复先验，提升退化条件下视觉融合为语义分支提供可靠感知证据的能力。2) 构建强多模态信息交互机制，由交互门控混合注意力（IGM-Att）与渐进耦合注意力（PC-Att）组成，从局部与全局视角保证场景表征完整。3) 构建视觉融合与语义融合的协同机制：通过特征搜索模块从两任务中提取互补关键特征，并以梯度校正策略从优化角度保障协作，通过一致性学习提升双方性能。

Result: 大量实验表明，W2F 在视觉观感与决策精度两方面均优于现有最先进方法。代码已开源。

Conclusion: W2F 通过视觉-语义互增强范式有效缓解了跨域优化鸿沟，实现视觉融合质量与下游语义决策精度的协同提升，为多模态图像融合提供了新思路。

Abstract: Existing multi-modal image fusion (MMIF) methods generally face an optimization gap between the visual and semantic domains, limiting the enhancement of perception capabilities. To overcome this long-standing barrier, this work proposes W2F, a new visual-semantic mutual reinforcing paradigm for MMIF. First, we reinforce the foundation for visual-semantic collaboration by amplifying feature complementarity through two targeted designs. Specifically, the segmentation-guided fusion loss introduces object-aware structural guidance into visual fusion, while the embedded information restoration prior strengthens the ability of visual fusion to provide reliable perceptual evidence for the semantic branch under degraded conditions. Then, we introduce a strong multi-modal information interaction mechanism composed of interactive gated mixed attention (IGM-Att) and progressive coupling attention (PC-Att). These modules ensure comprehensive scene representation from both local and global perspectives, providing informational support for visual-semantic collaboration. Finally, we construct a collaborative mechanism to couple visual fusion and semantic fusion. It employs a feature search module to extract complementary key features from both tasks, enriching the representational potential of visual and semantic information. Meanwhile, a gradient correction strategy ensures collaboration from the optimization perspective, enhancing performance for both visual and semantic fusion through consistency learning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that W2F surpasses state-of-the-art methods in both visual appeal and decision accuracy. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/HaoZhang1018/W2F.

</details>


### [25] [MeshBind: Point-to-Mesh Binding for Non-Rigid Scene Flow Automatic Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12895)
*Yinqi Chen,E Li,Shuaijun Wang,Weiyuan Peng,Meiying Zhang,Qi Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MeshBind自动标注方法，利用网格模型为非刚体目标生成逐点场景流，并发布SFB基准，微调后EPE降低2-3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶与机器人领域需要场景流估计，但行人等非刚体目标的真值场景流极难获取，缺乏带逐点非刚体标注的数据。

Method: 三项创新：1）多视图模块，从六个环视相机重建关节式网格模型并配准到LiDAR坐标系；2）局部坐标系绑定技术，通过逐三角切向-法向坐标系将点附着到网格，对刚体运动不变且适应关节形变；3）三层流配准流水线，结合自车运动补偿、框绑定与网格绑定生成全场景逐点流。同时在交互式标注工具SF-Studio中实现。

Result: 发布SFB（SUScape-Flow Benchmark），基于公开SUScape数据集，含4.1万帧逐点非刚体行人标注及Rigid/Semi-Rigid/Non-Rigid评测协议。实验显示在SFB上微调使三向EPE降低2-3倍，非刚体EPE达到与刚体EPE相当水平。

Conclusion: MeshBind实现了非刚体目标场景流的自动标注，SFB基准与评测协议验证了其有效性，显著提升非刚体场景流估计精度。

Abstract: Scene flow estimation is essential for many autonomous driving (AD) and robotic applications, but obtaining ground-truth scene flow for non-rigid objects such as pedestrians is highly difficult. We present MeshBind, an automatic annotation method that uses mesh models to generate per-point scene flow for non-rigid objects. The novelty of this work is threefold: 1) developing a multi-view module that reconstructs an articulated mesh model from six surround cameras and registers it to the LiDAR frame; 2) developing a local-frame binding technique that attaches each point to the mesh via a per-triangle tangent-normal frame, invariant under rigid motion yet adaptive to articulated deformation; 3) developing a three-layer flow registration pipeline that combines ego-vehicle motion compensation, box binding, and mesh binding to produce full-scene per-point flow. We implement MeshBind in SF-Studio, an interactive annotation tool that visualises flow and allows human mesh corrections with automatic recomputation. We also release SFB (SUScape-Flow Benchmark), a large-scale benchmark built on the public SUScape dataset, covering 41k frames with per-point non-rigid pedestrian annotations and a Rigid/Semi-Rigid/Non-Rigid evaluation protocol. Experiments show that fine-tuning on SFB reduces three-way endpoint error (EPE) by 2-3x, and brings non-rigid EPE to the same level as rigid EPE.

</details>


### [26] [AD-SRF: Attack-Defense Semantic Reinforcement for Multi-Modal Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12897)
*Hao Zhang,Zhiqian Zheng,Linfeng Tang,Xinyu Xiang,Jiayi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出攻击-防御语义强化框架AD-SRF，通过解耦视觉质量与语义属性的优化并无损重组，实现多模态图像融合中视觉质量与语义属性的双赢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像融合方法难以在视觉质量与语义属性之间取得平衡，原因在于二者的优化目标存在内在冲突。

Method: 1）构建基于Transformer的复原式视觉融合网络作为强视觉基线，配备跨模态检索模块与信息复原-融合协同损失，在退化条件下仍生成高可见度融合图像；2）引入攻击-防御博弈机制强化语义属性：攻击者学习损害下游分割的扰动模式，防御者从原始输入中恢复缺失语义并通过不可感知的语义图将其重整合进融合图像，经迭代对抗交互持续增强语义属性。

Result: 大量实验表明AD-SRF在视觉质量与语义属性上均取得双赢性能，下游分割性能提升且不损害视觉质量。代码已公开。

Conclusion: 通过解耦优化并无损重组视觉质量与语义属性，AD-SRF有效缓解二者冲突，为多模态图像融合提供了兼顾视觉与语义的解决方案。

Abstract: Existing image fusion methods struggle to balance visual quality and semantic attributes due to the inherent conflict between their optimization objectives. To address this challenge, we propose an attack-defense semantic reinforcement framework for multi-modal image fusion, termed AD-SRF. By decoupling the optimization of these two aspects and then losslessly reintegrating them, this framework injects rich semantic attributes into the fused image without compromising its visual quality. First, we develop a transformer-based restorative visual fusion network as a strong visual baseline. Equipped with the cross-modal retrieval module and the information restoration-fusion collaborative loss, it produces highly visible fused images even under degraded conditions. Second, we introduce an attack-defense game mechanism to strengthen the semantic attributes of the fused images. The attacker learns perturbation patterns that impair downstream segmentation, whereas the defender restores missing semantics from the original inputs and reintegrates them into the fused image through an imperceptible semantic map. Through iterative adversarial interaction, this mechanism drives the defender to continually strengthen the fused image's semantic attributes, boosting downstream segmentation performance without compromising visual quality. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AD-SRF achieves a win-win performance in both visual quality and semantic attributes. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/HaoZhang1018/AD-SRF.

</details>


### [27] [A Lightweight Velocity Correction Framework for Improving Instruction Following in Few-Step Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12899)
*Yating Gao,Zhenlong Zhu,Wen Zihao,Lenan Zhang,Jiexiong Wang,Yang Yang,Guanjun Jiang,Yixiong Zou,Takuya Maekawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个轻量级校正框架，在完全冻结的6B少步文生图生成器上，通过训练不到20M参数的速度残差校正器提升复杂提示的指令遵循能力，同时不改变采样日程与前向传播次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步文生图生成器虽能高质量快速出图，但在涉及多主体、交互、空间关系、计数和属性绑定的复杂提示上表现不佳。作者希望在不修改生成器参数、不增加前向传播次数的前提下减少这类错误。

Method: 冻结6B生成器，仅训练一个少于20M参数的校正器，在选定采样步学习对生成器预测速度的残差调整；校正器跨步共享并与生成器并行运行。为解决早期校正改变后续状态的问题，提出On-Trajectory Flow-Bridge Supervision，在修正生成实际到达的状态上推导残差目标。仅训练校正器，无需奖励优化或再蒸馏。

Result: 在目标复杂场景分布上，相比冻结的Z-Image-Turbo，成对指令遵循评估取得+17.60个百分点的NetWin，每张图仅增加26.9 ms（0.84%）端到端延迟，且骨干评估次数不增加。

Conclusion: 该框架据称是首个面向完全冻结少步文生图生成器的轻量级速度残差适配方法，保持原始采样日程与前向传播次数，在显著提升复杂场景指令遵循的同时保持低额外开销。

Abstract: Few-step text-to-image generators produce high-quality images with few network forward passes, yet struggle with complex prompts involving multiple subjects, interactions, spatial relations, counting, and attribute binding. Can these errors be reduced without changing the generator's parameters or increasing its forward-pass count? We propose a lightweight correction framework for a frozen 6B-parameter generator. The generator predicts velocities that determine how its intermediate image representation is updated; a corrector with fewer than 20M parameters learns residual adjustments at selected sampling steps. Because earlier corrections change the states encountered at later steps, our On-Trajectory Flow-Bridge Supervision derives residual targets at the states actually reached during corrected generation. A single corrector is shared across selected steps and runs in parallel with the generator. Only the corrector is trained, requiring neither reward optimization nor re-distillation. To our knowledge, this is the first framework for lightweight velocity-residual adaptation of fully frozen few-step text-to-image generators that preserves their original sampling schedule and forward-pass count. On our target complex-scene distribution, the framework achieves a +17.60-percentage-point NetWin over frozen Z-Image-Turbo in pairwise instruction-following evaluation, with only 26.9 ms (0.84%) additional end-to-end latency per image and no increase in backbone evaluations.

</details>


### [28] [JMT-YOLO: Multi-task Domain Adaptation via Segmentation-Guided Consistency Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12901)
*Zhenhua Yu,Jiahao Zhang,Wenchao Zhang,Ou Ye,Hengxiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出JMT-YOLO联合多任务学习框架，通过辅助语义分割任务与掩码机制提升域自适应目标检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 域自适应目标检测（DAOD）因源域与目标域间的分布偏移而性能下降。现有方法对细粒度特征建模不足、实例级对齐有限，在极端天气、目标遮挡等复杂环境下效果欠佳。

Method: 基于Mean Teacher架构，引入辅助语义分割任务与掩码机制：一是设计多尺度上下文感知分割头，结合密集连接结构捕捉局部与全局上下文特征，缓解细粒度特征退化；二是对目标域图像施加随机掩码并构建掩码一致性损失，引导网络学习结构上下文、增强实例级特征对齐。

Result: 在三个基准域自适应任务（Pascal VOC→Clipart1k、Cityscapes→Foggy Cityscapes、KITTI→Cityscapes）上分别取得46.9%、57.3%、61.0%的检测性能，优于现有最先进方法与基线。

Conclusion: JMT-YOLO通过联合多任务学习有效提升了复杂环境下的域自适应检测性能，验证了其有效性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Domain adaptive object detection (DAOD) aims to reduce performance degradation due to distribution shifts between training (source) and testing (target) datasets. However, owing to insufficient modeling of fine-grained features and limited instance-level alignment, existing approaches become less effective under complex environmental conditions, such as extreme weather conditions and object occlusion. We overcome these challenges by proposing JMT-YOLO, a novel joint multi-task learning framework for DAOD. JMT-YOLO is based on the Mean Teacher architecture. It introduces an auxiliary semantic segmentation task and a masking mechanism to enhance the modeling of foreground-background semantic boundaries in the target domain. Specifically, fine-grained feature degradation is reduced by introducing a multiscale context-aware segmentation head equipped with a dense connection structure to effectively capture local and global contextual features. A mask-consistency loss is formulated by applying random masking operations to target images, thereby guiding the network to learn structural context and improving instance-level feature alignment. Extensive experiments on three benchmark domain adaptation tasks (Pascal VOC to Clipart1k, Cityscapes to Foggy Cityscapes, and KITTI to Cityscapes) demonstrate that JMT-YOLO achieves detection performances of 46.9%, 57.3%, and 61.0%, respectively. These results demonstrate superior performance over existing state-of-the-art and baseline methods, thereby validating the effectiveness and generalization of JMT-YOLO.

</details>


### [29] [BLEND: Balancing Personalization vs. Generalization in Federated Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12907)
*Payam Abdisarabshali,Fardis Nadimi,Kasra Borazjani,Naji Khosravan,Richeng Jin,Seyyedali Hosseinalipour*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BLEND: 一种联邦视觉语言模型框架，通过全局/个性化适配器与融合损失，兼顾个性化与泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦视觉语言模型（FedVLMs）是新兴方向，但现有方法难以在联邦微调中同时提升个性化与泛化能力。

Method: 提出BLEND框架：采用选择性VLM参数微调与聚合策略，包含（i）跨客户端共享的全局视觉与文本适配器，（ii）每个客户端专属的个性化视觉投影适配器；并设计新损失函数，融合全局与个性化适配器输出以实现个性化，同时借助零样本VLM投影头构建有原则的锚点设计以促进泛化。

Result: 大量实验表明，BLEND在多样化系统设置下持续优于最先进基线，有效平衡个性化与泛化。

Conclusion: BLEND通过适配器分工与锚点损失设计，在联邦视觉语言模型中实现了个性化与泛化的联合增强。

Abstract: Federated vision-language models (FedVLMs) represent one of the emerging frontiers of machine learning, aiming to integrate federated learning within the fine-tuning pipelines of vision-language models (VLMs). In this work, we introduce BLEND, a new framework designed to jointly enhance the personalization and generalization capabilities of FedVLMs. BLEND proposes a selective VLM parameter fine-tuning and aggregation strategy that consists of (i) global vision and text adapters shared across clients, and (ii) a personalized vision projection adapter tailored to each client. BLEND further introduces a novel loss function that blends the outputs of the global and personalized adapters to achieve personalization, while simultaneously promoting generalization through a principled anchor design implemented via the projection heads of the zero-shot VLM. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BLEND consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, highlighting its ability to effectively balance personalization and generalization across diverse system settings. (Code is available at: https://github.com/payamsiabd/BLEND.git)

</details>


### [30] [What Egocentric Video Embeddings Encode and How to Combine Them with Inertial Sensors for Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12926)
*Kansuke Habano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 视频嵌入的主成分主要由相机朝向决定；肢体惯性信号承载的是与人无关、可跨人区分活动的成分。二者角色分离：视频负责检索同一动作组内的片段，惯性分类器负责打标签。用惯性预测在（去掉主成分的）被试内视频图上传播，使被试间交叉验证宏F1从0.648提升至0.852。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称视频与可穿戴惯性传感器常被同时采集，视频多用预训练模型编码，但在相机位于头部时这些嵌入到底表征了什么、以及应如何与惯性感知融合，缺少研究。

Method: 在 VideoMAEv2、CLIP、DINOv2 与第一人称 VC-1 上分析嵌入主方向；在 Ego-Exo4D、WEAR 上做集合级探针，区分"惯性可预测部分"与"剩余部分"的作用；在 WEAR 上打乱时间顺序、每个1秒片段仅随机取一个肢体，构建被试内视频图并移除其主导成分，将惯性预测沿该图传播（对比单一分类器融合、以及向图加入惯性边）。

Result: 各模型嵌入的主导主方向由"相机看向何处"决定：在 Ego-Exo4D 上头部朝向可解释第一人称（R^2 0.49–0.70）但不能解释第三人称相机，在 WEAR 上由场景布局解释。惯性信号可预测的部分能跨人区分活动；集合级探针中剩余部分能在被试内区分锻炼组、但无法跨人区分活动。在 WEAR 上传播使被试间交叉验证宏F1由0.648升至0.852；传播只对视频图有效，单分类器模态融合或图中加入惯性边均更差。

Conclusion: 视频与惯性在头部佩戴场景中承担互补角色：视频嵌入主要编码视角/场景，适于在同一集合内检索片段；惯性信号提供跨人可迁移的活动标签信息。最佳的模态结合方式不是在特征层融合，而是以惯性预测在被试内视频图上进行传播。

Abstract: Egocentric video and body-worn inertial sensors are increasingly recorded together, and the video is usually encoded by pretrained models. What these embeddings represent when the camera sits on the head, and how they should be combined with inertial sensing, has received little attention. Across VideoMAEv2, CLIP, DINOv2 and the egocentric VC-1, the leading principal directions of the embedding are dominated by where the camera looks. Measured head orientation explains them on Ego-Exo4D for egocentric (R^2 0.49-0.70) but not exocentric cameras, and the scene layout explains them on WEAR. The part that the limbs' inertial signals predict separates activities across people. In a set-level probe, the rest separates exercise sets within a participant but not activities across people. This is consistent with separate roles: the video finds the clips of the same set, and the inertial classifier labels them. On WEAR, with temporal order removed and one random limb per one-second clip, we propagate inertial predictions over a within-participant video graph whose leading components are removed. This raises subject-wise cross-validated macro-F1 from 0.648 to 0.852. In our comparisons, propagation helps only over the video graph, and fusing the modalities in one classifier, or adding inertial edges to the graph, performs worse.

</details>


### [31] [UniDeblur: Unified Motion Deblurring via Fourier Convolution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12941)
*Xintian Mao,Jiansheng Wang,Huaiyuan Wang,Qingli Li,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一运动去模糊框架Fourier Convolution Learning，通过傅里叶空间中的模糊自适应全局滤波学习，配合多源数据集与数据剪枝质量控制流程，实现跨相机、跨内容的统一去模糊，在域内及OOD基准上均取得竞争力表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去模糊方法主要针对单一场景专门化设计，面对不同相机和图像内容时性能显著下降。其根源在于去模糊网络通常依赖像素级映射，忽略了模糊的卷积本质，导致网络更多拟合图像内容本身而非学习卷积过程，难以处理多源模糊图像，因此亟需一个能应对多样模糊模式的统一去模糊框架。

Method: 1）构建多源数据集，并设计数据剪枝与质量控制流程；2）提出基于傅里叶卷积学习的统一运动去模糊框架；3）摒弃空间映射，转而在傅里叶空间进行模糊自适应的全局滤波学习，从而规避核估计中空间支撑受限的问题。

Result: 大量实验表明，该统一模型能有效处理来自不同源和场景的运动模糊，在域内与OOD基准上均达到具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 通过将去模糊建模为傅里叶空间的模糊自适应全局滤波学习，能够有效应对多源多样模糊模式，为统一运动去模糊提供了新思路；代码将在github.com/DeepMed-Lab-ECNU/Single-Image-Deblur发布。

Abstract: Image deblurring is a fundamental task in computer vision, yet existing methods mainly specialize in isolated scenarios. Consequently, the deblurring performance of these models drops significantly across scenarios with different cameras and image contents. This motivates the development of a unified motion deblurring framework that can handle diverse blur patterns. To this end, we construct a multi-source dataset and develop a data pruning and quality control pipeline. Then, to restore blur images with various motion blur kernels across different scenarios, we propose a unified motion deblurring framework via Fourier Convolution Learning. Existing deblurring networks usually rely on pixel-level mappings and overlook the convolutional nature of blur. Thus, the network mostly fits the image content itself rather than learning the convolution process, making it difficult to handle multi-source blur images. Instead of relying on spatial mapping, we perform blur-adaptive global filter learning in Fourier space, which avoids the limited spatial support of kernel estimation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our unified model effectively handles motion blurs across diverse sources and scenes, achieving competitive performance across both in-domain and OOD benchmarks. Code will be released at github.com/DeepMed-Lab-ECNU/Single-Image-Deblur.

</details>


### [32] [In-Context Learning Can Help Vision-Language Models Overcome Training Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12942)
*Kun Wang,Xindi Wu,Sanghyuk Chun,Olga Russakovsky,Esin Tureci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过受控的视觉上下文学习（in-context learning）证明：视觉语言模型在违反训练统计规律的样例上失败，并非缺乏视觉能力，而是先验主导；给予演示可将先验冲突的反事实样本准确率大幅提升（+27.2% / +15.1%），且对先验一致样本几乎无影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 在训练中习得强统计规律，当输入图像违反这些规律时，模型往往不能正确回应视觉证据。难点在于：难以区分这种"先验导致"的错误与"模型本身缺乏视觉能力"导致的错误。作者希望厘清失败根源，并据此提出更合理的评估范式。

Method: 采用受控的视觉上下文学习：在五个以视觉为中心的任务上，向模型提供演示样例，引导其关注视觉证据；对比先验冲突的反事实样本与先验一致样本的表现；覆盖五个 VLM 家族以检验泛化性；并通过分析视觉表征，考察注意力是否被重定向至 grounding 线索、对支撑先验的线索依赖是否降低；最后检验底层视觉能力薄弱或缺失时上下文学习的效果上限。

Result: 演示在先验冲突的反事实样本上带来大幅增益（Qwen3-VL 235B 提升 +27.2%，Qwen3-VL 32B 提升 +15.1%），而先验一致样本准确率几乎不变；结论在五个 VLM 家族上泛化，增益区间为 +9.7% 至 +30.3%。表征分析表明，演示确实将注意力重定向到 grounding 线索，同时减少对支持先验线索的依赖。此外，当底层视觉能力薄弱或缺失时，上下文学习效果受限。

Conclusion: 当前 VLM 实际具备被标准评估忽略的视觉 grounding 能力；此前观察到的失败多源于先验主导而非能力缺失。这凸显了采用受控评估设计来诊断与改进模型能力的必要性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) learn strong statistical regularities during training, which can make them fail to properly respond to visual evidence when input images violate those regularities. It is challenging to disambiguate these prior-induced errors from errors caused by the model missing the underlying visual capabilities. In this work, we use controlled visual in-context learning to encourage the model to focus on the visual evidence, and demonstrate that this alone is frequently sufficient to correct the errors -- suggesting that the models in fact do possess the relevant visual capabilities. Specifically, we show that across five vision-centric tasks, demonstrations yield large gains on prior-conflicting counterfactual examples ($+27.2\%$ for Qwen3-VL 235B and $+15.1\%$ for Qwen3-VL 32B) while leaving accuracy on prior-aligned examples nearly unchanged. These findings generalize across five VLM families with gains from $+9.7\%$ to $+30.3\%$. We analyze visual representations and find that demonstrations indeed redirect attention toward grounding cues while reducing reliance on cues that support the learned prior. Finally, we find that in-context learning is limited when the underlying visual capability is weak or absent. Together, these results show that current VLMs possess visual grounding capabilities that standard evaluations may overlook, underscoring the need for controlled evaluation design in diagnosing and improving model capability.

</details>


### [33] [Video Codec-Guided Training-Free Token Compression for Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12959)
*Xihua Sheng,Ruijie Liu,Chang Wen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种视频编解码器引导的免训练视觉token压缩方法，利用压缩视频中已有的编码信息作为低成本搜索先验，在保持性能的同时大幅降低视觉特征比较次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLLM依赖长视觉token序列理解复杂时序内容，推理效率低。现有免训练压缩方法虽减少送入语言模型的token数，但压缩过程本身（帧内token保护与帧间时序token合并）仍需大量视觉特征比较，计算开销大。

Method: 将参考帧依赖、运动矢量和残差对齐到视觉token网格。残差信息构建紧凑的候选池用于token保护；运动信息沿参考帧链传播，为稀疏采样帧间的时序token合并构建局部候选。随后仅在候选内进行局部视觉特征相似度比较以做出最终压缩决策，不修改基础VLLM。

Result: 在3个代表性VLLM和4个视频理解基准上，性能与最先进免训练基线FlashVID相当，某些设置下超过其表现，同时显著减少token保护和时序匹配所需的特征比较次数。

Conclusion: 编解码器信息能有效改善视频token压缩的精度-复杂度权衡。

Abstract: Video large language models (VLLMs) rely on long visual token sequences to understand complex temporal content, which makes visual token compression important for efficient inference. Existing training-free compression methods mainly reduce the number of tokens sent to the language model, but the compression process itself can still require many visual feature comparisons, especially for intra-frame token protection and inter-frame temporal token merging. This paper proposes a video codec-guided training-free token compression method for VLLMs that uses coding information already available in compressed video as a low-cost search prior. The method aligns reference-frame dependencies, motion vectors, and residuals with the visual token grid. Residual information forms compact candidate pools for token protection, while motion information is propagated along reference-frame chains to construct localized candidates for temporal token merging between sparsely sampled frames. Local visual feature similarity comparison is then used within these candidates to make the final compression decisions, without modifying the base VLLM. Experiments on three representative VLLMs and four video-understanding benchmarks show that the proposed method achieves performance comparable to the state-of-the-art training-free baseline FlashVID, and in some settings surpasses it, while substantially reducing the feature comparisons required by token protection and temporal matching. These results demonstrate that codec information can effectively improve the accuracy-complexity tradeoff of video token compression.

</details>


### [34] [MVGraph: Benchmarking LVLMs on Evidence Composition across Multi-View Visual Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12961)
*Yingjie Zhu,Han Cheng,Xuefeng Bai,Youcheng Pan,Yang Xiang,Kehai Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MVGraph是一个面向大视觉语言模型多图推理的基准，包含8k实例、8类图论任务与两个多图场景，并配套29项诊断任务评估感知、对齐、组合和推理四项能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多图推理基准局限于单一自包含结构，仅评估图感知与推理，未探索跨视图证据的对齐与组合。而实际中所需证据常分布于不同来源图、时间快照或局部观测中。

Method: 构建MVGraph基准：8k实例覆盖8类图论任务，抽象自部分图视图和时间图视图两种多图场景；每个问题程序化构造并验证单视图不可解。将多图推理分解为感知、对齐、组合、推理四项能力，设计29项诊断任务（64k实例）及黄金中间表示以评估中间过程。

Result: 在15个LVLM上评测，揭示多图推理的难度，并通过阶段导向诊断识别出跨视图边对齐与关系级组合为主要瓶颈；时间约束和视图数量增加进一步暴露能力边界。

Conclusion: MVGraph为多图推理提供系统性评估框架，明确跨视图对齐与关系级组合是关键瓶颈，代码与数据已公开。

Abstract: We introduce MVGraph, a benchmark for evaluating large vision-language models (LVLMs) on multi-graph reasoning where the evidence required by a query is distributed across multiple interrelated visual graph views. Although such evidence commonly resides in source-specific graphs, temporal snapshots, or local observations, existing graph reasoning benchmarks remain confined to a single self-contained structure, assessing only graph perception and reasoning while leaving the alignment and composition of evidence across views underexplored. MVGraph comprises 8k instances spanning 8 \textit{graph-theoretic} tasks, abstracted from two multi-graph scenarios (partial and temporal graph views). Each problem is programmatically constructed and verified to be unsolvable from a single view. Beyond end-to-end evaluation, we decompose multi-graph reasoning into four complementary capabilities---Perception, Alignment, Composition, and Reasoning---and design 29 diagnostic tasks with 64k instances to probe each one, along with golden intermediate representations for evaluating the intermediate process. Across 15 LVLMs, we reveal the difficulty of multi-graph reasoning and identify cross-view edge alignment and relation-level composition as prominent bottlenecks through stage-oriented diagnostics. Meanwhile, temporal constraints and increasing view counts expose further capability boundaries. Code and data are available at https://github.com/AAAndy-Zhu/MVGraph.

</details>


### [35] [Learning Representations to Guide Pixel Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12978)
*Zihao Pan,Mengting Chen,Cheng Shi,Taihang Hu,Zhengrong Yue,Zikang Wang,Yuan Wang,Wei Li,Sibei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究揭示像素扩散Transformer中表征质量与生成效用不必然正相关，提出表征引导（RG）方法，通过低维瓶颈与流匹配目标的显式引导，在ImageNet 256x256上以459M参数超越更大模型，FID达1.55。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉表征主要为"理解"任务设计，何种表征对"生成"有用尚不清晰；作者试图厘清像素扩散Transformer中表征与生成效用之间的关系。

Method: 基于两个核心发现（外部对齐语义在像素合成中会逐步丢失；架构解耦与专用像素解码器主要重新分配语义与空间建模），提出表征引导（Representation Guidance）：用低维瓶颈将生成相关信息压缩为紧凑表征，并通过其诱导的预测偏移修改流匹配（flow-matching）目标，显式引导解码器生成，同时保持朴素Transformer架构。

Result: RG在参数与计算上高效扩展，学到更清晰的语义与空间结构；ImageNet 256x256上RG-L/16（仅459M参数）优于显著更大的像素扩散Transformer，RG-XL/16的FID为1.55；512x512下FID为1.74；扩展至文本到图像生成，GenEval达0.89，DPG-Bench达87.1。

Conclusion: 有用的生成表征必须同时"被正确形成"和"被主动利用"；RG通过形成并利用内部表征，在保持简单架构的同时实现高效、强竞争力的生成性能，并可推广至更高分辨率与文本到图像任务。

Abstract: Visual representations are largely developed for understanding, yet what makes them useful for generation remains unclear. We study this question in pixel diffusion transformers and uncover two key insights. First, better representation quality does not necessarily translate into greater generative utility, as externally aligned semantics can be progressively lost during pixel synthesis.
